# 3D building data for Africa, Oceania and the global datasets (task #1328)

Run date 2026-11-13. Read-only research (task #1328) for Scenic Roads, with the index over all six buildings reports in buildings-index.md. Method: four sub-agents (Sonnet, medium) by area (Africa north, west and central; Africa east and southern with the Indian Ocean islands; Australia; New Zealand, the Pacific and Antarctica), each fetching pages and API answers with User-Agent `scenic-roads/0.1 (personal offline map)` and measuring the opened files against Overture; I took the global datasets, the 30-box Overture sample and the four-box comparison of global height sets myself. Each source row, note and list was written by hand and edited into the current statement in the sections below; every table row was checked for its header's cell count (0 mismatches; the overlap tables have their own columns). Overture release for every figure: 2026-10-21.0. The regional reports (Europe, Asia, the Americas, USA) are separate files.

## 1. Headline

- **What was measured from opened files and what is described from pages only.** Measured against Overture release 2026-10-21.0 from real source data: Queensland's LiDAR building outlines (Brisbane, Gold Coast), City of Melbourne's 2023 footprints, Ballarat, Geelong, Launceston, Christmas Island and Cocos (Keeling); LINZ's building outlines, Christchurch City Council, Queenstown Lakes, Wellington City Council (footprints and 3D suburb models) and Tauranga; Samoa, Palau, Guam and French Polynesia footprints; IGN BD TOPO for Réunion and Mayotte, the City of Cape Town's two layers and Kigali's EO4Kigali set; the City of Adelaide's 3D model (indicative) and eThekwini's footprints; and, for the global sets, Google's 2.5D Temporal raster, GHS-BUILT-H, 3D-GloBFP and one GlobalBuildingAtlas tile against trusted heights in Berlin Mitte, central Melbourne, central Cape Town and Midtown Manhattan (section 9.5), plus 3D-GloBFP against Overture in 13 African boxes (section 9.1). Described from pages only: Geoscape, Vicmap Buildings, NSW's models, GlobalBuildingAtlas beyond that one tile, WSF 3D, GHS-OBAT, GLAMOUR, GHS-BUILT-V, every African national source (none exists openly), and the paid vendors. Sections 9 and 10 give the figures and the gaps.
- **Where the sources add most.** Africa and the Pacific have almost nothing open per building: Overture's heights in 14 African city boxes are OSM tags on 0.0 % to 9.7 % of buildings (Johannesburg 9.7 %, Casablanca 4.1 %, Lagos 1.2 %, Accra 0.0 %), and in the Pacific on 0.1 % (Suva) and 24.3 % (Port Moresby, OSM); in New Zealand's three big city boxes 0.2 % to 1.7 %. The real gains are in a few places: Queensland's LiDAR outlines add a height to 25.3 % of Overture's buildings in the Brisbane box and 17.7 % on the Gold Coast (ridge heights run 1.60 and 1.81 times Overture's median; eave heights agree, ratio 0.91 on the Gold Coast); City of Melbourne's 2023 footprints add one to 37.9 % (ratio 0.95); Launceston to 65.8 %; Ballarat to 22.4 %; Christchurch City Council's LiDAR-derived heights (237,094 buildings, CC BY 4.0) to 65 % of Overture's buildings in its box; Queenstown Lakes to 74 %; IGN's BD TOPO to 55.5 % in Saint-Denis (Réunion) and 51.2 % in Mamoudzou (Mayotte); the City of Cape Town's photogrammetric layer to 57.5 % in its CBD box (non-commercial, no redistribution); Kigali's set to 44.2 %; the City of Adelaide's 3D model to 71 % of the buildings it covers (indicative: the model has no stated coordinate system and was registered by inference). Geelong adds 0.9 % because Overture already carries its heights through an OSM import.
- **Nothing national and open for heights or storeys anywhere in Africa, and almost nowhere in Oceania.** Geoscape's national buildings (19,159,278 in the September 2026 release, a height record on 82.1 %) are commercial; Vicmap Buildings and NSW's supply are government-only; the Land Information New Zealand outlines have no height or storeys (3.27 M features, CC BY 4.0); GNS's Otago inventory of storeys is not shareable. Tasmania's LIST, the ACT, WA's DPIRD set and Hobart's footprints carry no height.
- **Overture in the sample (section 8).** Australia's heights come mostly from Microsoft ML: Brisbane 66.6 %, Darwin 65.7 %, Canberra 51.2 %, Hobart 44.7 %, Perth 40.2 %, Adelaide 38.9 %, Sydney 16.5 %, Melbourne 14.4 %, rural NSW 90.0 %. Storeys are OSM tags (Sydney 64.9 %, Adelaide 39.5 %, Melbourne 9.9 %). New Zealand's heights are OSM only (Auckland 1.7 %, Christchurch 1.1 %, Wellington 0.2 %) and 92.1 %, 92.9 % and 73.7 % of its buildings in the three city boxes have neither a height nor storeys. In Africa 73.0 % (Cape Town) to 99.9 % (rural Kenya) of buildings have neither a height nor storeys, except Dar es Salaam (34.0 %, an OSM storey effort).
- **Which global fallback is best (section 9.5: four boxes against trusted heights; section 9.1: 13 African boxes).** No global set measures well on individual buildings, and the answer differs by continent. Where Overture already holds a Microsoft ML height it is the closest building-level value (Berlin Mitte: median absolute difference 3.97 m, ratio 0.84, 64.4 % within 20 % on 90 pairs; central Melbourne: 2.45 m, 0.88, 31.4 % on 695 pairs), but it exists on 13.5 % to 17.1 % of the trusted buildings in those boxes and on 51 of 6,483 in Midtown. **Europe, Australia, New Zealand:** next best is the GlobalBuildingAtlas LoD1 height (Berlin tile only: 3.08 m low, ratio 0.84, 19.9 % within 20 %, correlation 0.60; CC BY-NC 4.0), then 3D-GloBFP, which overestimates in dense centres (median +8.29 m in Berlin Mitte, ratio 1.65; +6.41 m in Melbourne, ratio 1.92). **North America:** 3D-GloBFP's block-sized polygons in Midtown Manhattan run +43.69 m (ratio 3.12) and GHS-BUILT-H 1.27 times, so Overture's imported OSM heights (identical to NYC's layer there) are the fallback. **Africa, South and South-East Asia, Latin America:** Google's 2.5D Temporal raster is closest where it covers (Cape Town, 909 buildings: median absolute 2.24 m, ratio 0.80, correlation 0.84, 38.6 % within 20 %); 3D-GloBFP covers every African country and territory and gives a height to 49 % to 99 % of Overture's buildings that have neither height nor storeys in 12 of 13 African boxes (Bamako 99 %, Kinshasa 96 %, Casablanca 81 %, Cairo 81 %, Tunis 80 %, Abuja 79 %; Las Palmas 20 %), with median ratios against OSM heights from 0.40 (Khartoum) to 2.18 (Algiers); in Cape Town it has no bias (+0.43 m). It is the only global per-building source for Morocco, Western Sahara and Libya, where 2.5D does not cover. GHS-BUILT-H is a 100 m average (per building ratio 0.85, 2.13, 1.54 and 1.27 in the four boxes; 12.0 % to 20.6 % within 20 %): a last resort or a sanity check. A proposed order is in the index report.
- **Licences for a personal offline cache.** Open and attribution-only (cacheable, mixable with ODbL data with credit): LINZ outlines, Christchurch, Queenstown Lakes, City of Melbourne, Ballarat, Launceston, Brisbane's and Queensland's LiDAR outlines per the Queensland rows, BD TOPO (Licence Ouverte), Kigali (CC BY 4.0), Google 2.5D and Open Buildings (CC BY 4.0 or ODbL, chosen), GHS-BUILT-H (CC BY 4.0), 3D-GloBFP (CC BY 4.0), WSF 3D (CC BY 4.0), GHS-OBAT (CC BY 4.0). Not usable or not open: Geoscape, Vicmap Buildings, NSW and WA outlines (government-only or fee), GNS Otago (restricted), Google Photorealistic 3D Tiles (offline and derivative uses excluded), paid vendors; GRID3 (CC BY-SA 4.0) and WorldPop Nigeria (CC BY-NC-SA) are share-alike or non-commercial; the City of Cape Town's layers are non-commercial with no redistribution (a personal cache is fine, they cannot be mixed into an ODbL set); GlobalBuildingAtlas is non-commercial (CC BY-NC 4.0) apart from its ODbL polygon part.
- **Unverified and why (section 10).** NSW's building models (no anonymous building file in 374 Spatial Services records; the Cowra scene needs a signed key), Victoria's live pages and Sydney's Right to Know reply (Cloudflare challenges, not bypassed), data.govt.nz and the Pacific Data Hub (bot checks), the national geoportals of PNG, Fiji, Samoa, Tonga, Vanuatu, Solomon Islands, Kiribati and Tuvalu (no address on any fetched page), Te Fenua and Georep (script pages), eight South African provincial and metro portals (no connection, 502 or no building layer found), the energydata.info rooftop-solar footprints for Addis Ababa, Nairobi, Dar es Salaam and Johannesburg (Cloudflare), GlobalBuildingAtlas files outside Europe (Hugging Face login; the mediaTUM record prints server passwords, which are not used), Esri and Maxar terms (pages truncated, 403 or redirected) and the Canary, Ceuta and Melilla Catastro zips (certificate chain does not verify).

## 2. Africa: region tables

Each row is a source or a country with nothing national. Where a country row says "Nothing national found", the evidence is the HDX country search (section 6) plus the portals named in the row.

| Europe: EUBUCCO | 30 countries; v0.1 paper, later versions | EUBUCCO team (TU Berlin and others) | 322 million+ footprints with type, subtype, height, floors, construction year (15.9 %) | Height and floor values from registries (62.2 % of footprints), merged Microsoft (20.4 %) and OSM (17.4 %) data, or machine-learning estimates | EU-27, UK, Norway, Switzerland; attribute completeness stated as 100 % for type, subtype, height, floors on the home page | Per country | Download page; WKB geometry, EPSG:3035 | Licence not on the home page; the Europe reports read it (see buildings-europe-w.md and -EUROPE-E.md) | https://eubucco.com/ | Europe only; see the Europe reports | https://eubucco.com/ |
| Global: OpenStreetMap itself (building:levels, height, roof tags; Simple 3D Buildings) | Live database; schema on the wiki | OpenStreetMap contributors | `height` (distance from lowest ground contact to roof top, excluding antennas, spires and roof equipment), `min_height`, `building:levels` (above ground, excluding roof levels; "use building:levels only in addition to a height tag"), `roof:levels`, `roof:shape` (flat, skillion, gabled, hipped, pyramidal, gambrel, mansard, dome, onion, round, saltbox and others), `building:part` for parts | Mapper-entered; no stated accuracy | Varies by place: the sample in section 8 has OSM-supplied heights on 0.0 % to 9.7 % of buildings in the African city boxes and on 0.1 % to 1.7 % in the New Zealand and Fiji boxes | Continuous | Planet files, extracts, Overpass | ODbL 1.0. Cache: yes; it is the ODbL source | https://wiki.openstreetmap.org/wiki/Simple_3D_Buildings | Already the primary Overture source | https://wiki.openstreetmap.org/wiki/Simple_3D_Buildings |
| Global: Google Photorealistic 3D Tiles | Map Tiles API | Google | Photogrammetric 3D Tiles mesh (glTF) of cities | Mesh; no building semantics | Cities worldwide | Rolling | 3D Tiles over HTTPS | Not cacheable for offline use: the policies page says to cache or store content "only as expressly permitted", offline uses "are excluded", no bulk download or scraping, and "Don't create content based on Google Maps Content" (building 3D or terrain models from it is named as prohibited). Not usable; not ODbL-mixable | https://developers.google.com/maps/documentation/tile/policies | Excluded by its terms | https://developers.google.com/maps/documentation/tile/policies |
| Global (commercial): Esri 3D basemap and Community Maps, Vexcel Building Attributes and 3D Cities, Maxar (Vantor) Precision3D / Vivid Terrain, Nearmap AI | Vendor products | Esri, Vexcel, Maxar (now Vantor), Nearmap | Footprints with heights, 3D meshes (Vexcel 3D Tiles; Maxar Precision3D) | Photogrammetry or AI from aerial imagery (search results only; no vendor page opened) | Selected cities and countries | Rolling | Paid, vendor delivery | Vexcel 3D City EULA (January 2024) read: a perpetual, revocable, non-exclusive, non-transferable licence to use, reproduce and modify the product for internal use only (no commercial purpose: no redistribution or publication); copying, display and distribution to others are barred; paid. Esri and Maxar (now Vantor): the pages answered 403, a truncated page of headings and a redirect, so their terms are not read. Not usable without a contract | https://vexceldata.com/wp-content/uploads/2024/02/2024.01.31-3D-City-EULA.pdf | Out of scope: paid; Overture already carries Esri Community Maps footprints (CC BY 4.0) | https://vexceldata.com/wp-content/uploads/2024/02/2024.01.31-3D-City-EULA.pdf |

## 5. Notes per jurisdiction

### 5.1 Africa: north, west and central

- **Morocco and Western Sahara.** data.gov.ma is CKAN; its API answered 14 hits for "bâtiment" (heritage-site lists, sector indicators, a solar-heater guide) and zero for building, cadastre, footprint. ANCFCC is named in search results as the national cadastre and mapping authority but publishes no building layer I could find. The ITOS Al Haouz layer (16k+ digitized structures, CC BY) and the Google quake extract cover the earthquake area only. Not opened. Google 2.5D Temporal does not list Morocco, so Casablanca and Rabat have no height source beyond OSM (Casablanca 1.1 % height, Rabat 6.6 %).
- **Algeria, Tunisia, Libya, Egypt, Sudan.** No national or city product found. data.gov.tn answers on its home page (theme list) but its search pages return HTTP 500. Egypt: no ESA product found; a UN-GGIM slide set (search result) mentions an ESA New Cairo pilot with 3D models and DSMs, with no public release found. Libya: only the Derna flood extract. Sudan: Khartoum shows low 2.5D coverage (50 % of the box).
- **Sahel and West Africa.** Nothing national in any of the 15 countries (HDX country searches). Open Cities Africa ran projects in Accra, Monrovia and Saint-Louis here (OSM footprints, drone imagery). World Bank energydata.info holds Liberia digitized structures (CC BY 4.0), Accra and Lagos rooftop-solar footprints (CC0): file downloads sit behind a Cloudflare challenge from here and were not opened (not bypassed). Ghana: the World Bank building predictions are rasters (no vectors); Colouring Ghana is a planned platform.
- **Nigeria.** The NGDI portal and the Surveyor-General's data are not open. GRID3 NGA v4.1 is the national "building-adjacent" set (blocks and 2.5D height summaries, CC BY-SA 4.0). Lagos, Kano and Abuja have no state or FCT open building download found. Abuja: AGIS calls itself the only official geospatial source for FCT; an OSGOF drone-imagery and 3D city-view project for Abuja appears in news items only.
- **Central Africa.** Nothing national for Cameroon, CAR, Equatorial Guinea, Gabon, Congo or DR Congo. Kinshasa has World Bank building points (survey design) and GRID3 COD v4.0 blocks; Overture's Kinshasa box is 83 % Google footprints.
- **Islands.** Sao Tome and Principe and Cabo Verde: nothing beyond HOT exports (2.0 MB and 11 MB). Canary Islands: Catastro INSPIRE feeds for provinces 35 and 38 exist (read through Wayback, updated 2026-02-21); the host's certificate chain does not verify from this Mac or the mini, so no zip was downloaded and storeys on the Canary buildings are inferred from the Spain row, not measured.

### 5.2 Africa: east, southern and the islands

Fetched: the pages and API answers named per entry; "OPENED" means a real file or query answer was read and measured here, "pages only" means described from catalogue or page text.

- **Africa-wide open sets (all countries).** HDX CKAN (package_search per country group, run for 29 groups) lists for every country in my area a HOT OSM buildings export (ODbL, GeoPackage/GeoParquet/Shapefile/PMTiles; Kenya record 2 Nov 2026, South Africa 15 Oct 2026) and a GRID3 Settlement Extents v3.0 record (CC BY-SA; polygons of settlements, not buildings; not listed for South Sudan). Google Open Buildings v3 country records exist on HDX for Ethiopia, Somalia, South Sudan, Kenya, Burundi, Malawi, Zambia, Zimbabwe, Mozambique and Madagascar (ODbL); Google's own page says v3 covers Africa and more, inferred May 2023, rooftop polygons with a confidence score, no height, CC BY 4.0 or ODbL 1.0 at the user's choice. Pages only for the country records; OPENED: none (hundreds of MB each; their content, OSM and Google footprints, is what Overture already carries).
- **Google Open Buildings 2.5D Temporal.** Earth Engine catalogue and HDX record: annual 2016-2023 rasters for Africa, South and South-East Asia and Latin America, 0.5 m raster grid with a stated effective resolution of 4 m from a Sentinel-2 model, band building_height in metres relative to terrain (0-100 m), plus building_presence and fractional count; CC BY 4.0 or ODbL 1.0; no accuracy figure for the height band is published on the page. HDX zip (48 MB) holds .txt lists for 130+ countries (the tiles are fetched by script). Pages only. This is the only height layer that spans all my countries, at a coarse 4 m scale.
- **GlobalBuildingAtlas (TUM).** ESSD article: polygons (2.75 billion), 3 m height raster and LoD1 models for 2.68 billion buildings, global; licence not stated as one licence (article CC BY 4.0, the OSM and Microsoft polygon portion ODbL); the article says no reference data existed for Africa, so no quantitative evaluation there. mediaTUM DOI given. Pages only; not downloaded (8 GB).
- **South Africa.** The national CD:NGI portal loads by script; its product page lists vector topographic data, a photogrammetric 25 m DEM and 5 m contours but names no buildings layer, no licence and no price. The best sources are municipal: Cape Town (two ArcGIS layers, both OPENED with real queries; the 2023 LiDAR layer carries a mean building height in metres and is the only metro-wide LiDAR height set found, 1,444,950 polygons), eThekwini (846,698 footprints, no height), Ekurhuleni (Google copies), Johannesburg (2023 LiDAR held, offered on request only; its 2024 inventory shows no building layer). Cape Town's licence field gives non-commercial use with acknowledgment and no redistribution, which allows a personal offline cache but not an ODbL merge.
- **Rwanda, Kigali.** EO4Kigali (Zenodo 3239271, CC BY 4.0) is the one openable authoritative-style set: OPENED, 224,657 polygons with a Height field from Pleiades imagery; the heights look like single-storey roofs (median 2.1 m), so treat as a coarse proxy.
- **Tanzania and Zanzibar.** The Zanzibar Commission for Lands UAV outlines (CC BY 4.0, credit ZMI and the Government of Zanzibar) are in OSM already; Ramani Huria's ward mapping is OSM. No height in either. World Bank rooftop-solar samples for Dar es Salaam, Nairobi, Addis Ababa and four South African towns exist on energydata.info (CC0 or CC BY 4.0); the zip downloads sit behind a Cloudflare challenge and were not fetched, so pages only.
- **Uganda.** KCCA's Kawempe data (35,538 buildings, 2011-2016) was imported to OSM; no height.
- **Reunion and Mayotte (France).** BD TOPO V3 through the Geoplateforme WFS (2026-09-15 release): OPENED for two boxes; hauteur in metres from photogrammetric z where z exists, Licence Ouverte. For Reunion and Mayotte this is the best source found and a strong height gain over Overture (section 9).
- **Seychelles.** OSM import page: outlines digitized by the Centre for GIS from a high-resolution aerial photo, with a residential flag from the National Bureau of Statistics; pages only.
- **Countries with only the Africa-wide sets.** Eritrea, Djibouti, Somalia, South Sudan, Burundi, Malawi, Zambia, Zimbabwe, Mozambique, Angola, Namibia, Botswana, Eswatini, Lesotho, Madagascar, Mauritius, Comoros, Saint Helena group, BIOT, French Southern Territories: no national or city portal with buildings was reached in this run (the portal addresses came from search results that did not lead to a buildings layer; see F).

### 5.3 Australia

Fetched: the pages and API answers named in each note. OPENED means a real file or live query result was read; PAGES means a description only.

- **Commonwealth (PAGES).** Geoscape's Buildings is the only national product with heights (roof, eave, volume, estimated levels) and it is commercial. The September 2026 release (v4.1, 19,159,278 buildings: ACT 257,972, NSW 5,335,620, NT 166,718, OT 457, QLD 3,808,687, SA 1,759,499, TAS 604,693, VIC 5,042,226, WA 2,183,406; its building_height table has 15,720,277 records, 82.1 %) is the current National Buildings release. Heights exist only where the building was captured from aerial imagery (urban areas); satellite and rural records carry outlines. Public access is limited to one-SA2 samples (June 2022, behind AURIN) and a NSW government-only supply. NEXIS was decommissioned on 17 June 2025 and only ever published LGA and SA1 aggregates. The Building Geometry Model is a tool, not data.
- **New South Wales (PAGES).** No open building outline or LoD2 layer was found on the data.gov.au mirror of the NSW portal (all Spatial Services records with building, 3D, mesh, twin, LiDAR, roof, LoD in the title were listed: scene layers for addresses, strata, places, trees, railways, two reality meshes and one 10 m BIM building). A Strata 3D record mentions 3D Buildings (LoD 2.0 from LiDAR) as an associated resource, but no such dataset is in the catalogue and its licence is unstated. The Geoscape supply is for NSW government agencies and councils only. The Spatial Digital Twin's own service root answered `MissingKey` (signed access), so the I3S layers were not enumerated.
- **Victoria (PAGES for Vicmap Buildings; OPENED for Vicmap Features).** Vicmap Buildings (LOD0-LOD2, statewide at varying detail, built from Vicmap Imagery and Elevation plus commercially licensed data) is available to Victorian public-sector, emergency-management and local-government users through the Coordinated Imagery Program by e-mail request. The land.vic.gov.au page returns a Cloudflare challenge to curl (HTTP 403, not bypassed); the text is from Internet Archive captures (id_ form; a request for the 2026-07-31 capture was redirected by the archive to a December 2025 one). The open Vicmap Features building_polygon holds 32,604 larger-building polygons and no heights. Open height data in Victoria is council-level: Melbourne, Geelong, Ballarat, Yarra (3DS).
- **Queensland (OPENED).** The best open source in Australia for state-scale heights. The Building outlines [generated] layer is the LiDAR-derived (building class 6) outline set made for QRA damage assessment: 3,544,741 outlines over 120 project areas (3,069,378 flagged Current; a project that was re-flown keeps its older outlines flagged Superseded, so filter on status = Current: Brisbane has 2019 superseded rows plus 2025 current rows covering the same buildings). Every row has bsm_min, bsm_max, bsm_edge and dtm_centre. Heights are elevation differences (roof maximum or average eave elevation minus ground at the centroid), so a ridge, plant room or parapet sets bsm_max; the ridge value ran 3.2 m above Overture's heights in both boxes while the eave value matched (Gold Coast median -0.35 m). Queried through spatial-gis.information.qld.gov.au; data.qld.gov.au and gisservices.qra.qld.gov.au answered an AWS WAF challenge and HTTP 403 and were not used (the dataset page came through the data.gov.au mirror). No storeys.
- **Western Australia (OPENED metadata; no file).** Open: DPIRD Buildings of WA, 1,119,752 coarse polygons, CC BY 4.0, no heights. Landgate's deep-learning outlines (2021-2026) are marked access_level govt_only; its topographic building polygons are fee-based under the Personal Use Licence. No 3D or height product found for Perth; the City of Perth's CPS2 layers are planning height limits.
- **South Australia (PAGES plus zip listing).** DEW's Urban Buildings 2022 is a LiDAR-classified raster (140 MB TIF) with no height; the 2022 report's glossary defines it as horizontal extent. The City of Adelaide 3D model is block-wise 3DS (2010-2014), a graphics model, not a joinable layer. City of Salisbury's Building Heights shapefile (2014) returns 404.
- **Tasmania (OPENED).** The LIST's 477,077 footprints carry a MEAN_HGT field that is empty everywhere (service count of non-null = 0, the product sheet says the same). Council layers do better: Launceston's Buildings (74,059, Height_m on 70,246, CC BY 4.0) is a survey database whose dates run mostly 1985-1997; Hobart has footprints and YearConstruction only.
- **Australian Capital Territory (OPENED).** Static 64,672 footprints, CC BY 4.0, no heights. ACTmapi publishes LiDAR but no building-height layer was found.
- **Northern Territory (nothing found).** data.nt.gov.au (CKAN) returned one building-related record (a habitat-mapping program) and no footprint, LiDAR or 3D datasets; Darwin appears only in Geoscape (the SA2 sample and a Darwin Municipality update in the March 2026 release, closed) and in Overture.
- **City of Melbourne (OPENED).** The best Australian city source: 41,701 stacked-tier footprints with structure_max/min elevation and extrusion on every row (AHD), 2023 release, CC BY. Heights are roof-top above the structure's lowest elevation, so a podium-and-tower stack repeats the whole structure's extrusion on each tier (the measurement counts each polygon). The 2 cm photomesh (2018, 2020) and 2018 point cloud are separate downloads.
- **Geelong, Ballarat, Launceston (OPENED).** Three council layers with real per-building heights. Geelong's roofprints and Ballarat's 3D buildings were imported into OSM (OSM wiki import plans cite data.gov.au explicit permission), which is why Overture's Geelong heights equal the council's: nothing to gain there. Ballarat gains because only part of the import landed.
- **External territories (OPENED).** Christmas Island (911) and Cocos (561) have GA outlines with DSM minus DEM heights (2011 imagery, CC BY 3.0 AU). Norfolk Island, Heard and McDonald Islands and the Australian Antarctic Territory: no building dataset found; Overture has 3,035 OSM buildings in the Norfolk box and none with heights.
- **Capital and other cities not listed above.** Sydney (hub search for building, footprint, 3D, height, model, LiDAR, storey, massing: no building dataset; the NSW and Geoscape rows apply), Perth, Darwin, Canberra, Newcastle, Wollongong: nothing open beyond what the state rows say (section 6).

### 5.4 New Zealand, the Pacific and Antarctica

Fetched: the LINZ layer record and version list, the ArcGIS REST roots and layers named in sections 2 to 4, the ArcGIS Online item records, the HDX package search, SPREP, data.gouv.fr and the Microsoft tile index.

#### New Zealand
- LINZ NZ Building Outlines is the only national source: footprints, no heights, no storeys. OPENED through its ArcGIS FeatureServer copy (public, no key): 3,268,141 features counted there against 3,236,141 in the LDS layer record (the copies differ by 32,000, cause not checked). The LDS layer lists update versions every few weeks by region (latest 2026-05-18 for Hawke's Bay rural areas). Related layers named in the LDS description: NZ Building Outlines (All Sources) (layer 101292) and a Lifecycle table (101291, no longer updated past January 2025); neither was opened.
- Overture in New Zealand is OpenStreetMap-only in every box measured (Auckland, Wellington, Christchurch, Tauranga, Queenstown, Dunedin: footprint sources OpenStreetMap plus Esri Community Maps, a few percent). LINZ is therefore a footprint add, not a height add; 10 to 45 % of Overture buildings in the city boxes have no LINZ outline under them.
- Heights exist only from councils: Christchurch (drvLiDARHeight, open CC BY, best find), Queenstown Lakes (bldgheight, eaveheight, roofform, CC BY), Wellington (64 suburb 3D models, CC BY 4.0, height only in the geometry; its footprints' `approx_hei` is a rooftop elevation above mean sea level, not a height), Tauranga (Z, licence absent), Otago (Storeys, restricted). Auckland Council publishes a photogrammetric mesh of the CBD only (no attributes).
- Quirks: Wellington's `approx_hei` is defined in the data.govt.nz record as the rooftop elevation above mean sea level (Wellington 1953): in the CBD it reads like a height, on hillsides it is an elevation, and `approx_hei` minus `base_elev` is negative on 62 % of the box, so the footprints give no usable height. Tauranga's Z is likewise undocumented. Christchurch's heights are a derived LiDAR attribute, consent-based heights are empty. GNS/Otago is explicitly not to be redistributed.
- Not downloaded: the Wellington 3D files (GDB, DWG, SKP on an S3 bucket) and the Auckland and Tauranga scene layers. Auckland's CBD mesh is view-only ("not available for download"); its region DSM 2024 (1 m, LiDAR 8 points per square metre, CC BY 4.0) gives derived heights only.
- data.govt.nz (the national catalogue) answers a bot check ("Pardon Our Interruption") to curl and to the fetch tool: no page of it was read, nothing was done to get round it. Council portal addresses were found through ArcGIS Online instead.

#### Pacific
- Every Pacific country has a HOT OSM export on HDX (buildings, updated 2026-11-05 to 2026-11-10) and Overture there is OSM-only (Overture boxes: Suva 8,285, Port Moresby 3,073, Noumea 9,452, Papeete 7,594, Guam 13,825, Saipan 12,834, Pago Pago 3,126, Majuro 936, Tarawa 10,625, Honiara 15,865, Nuku'alofa 15,259, Port Vila 16,761, Rarotonga 7,080, Niue 1,054, Wallis 3,719, Pitcairn 165). Heights are near zero (Port Moresby alone has 720 of 3,073 from OSM mapping); storeys a few percent.
- Official footprint sets found and OPENED: Samoa 2009 (SPREP), Palau 2019 (PALARIS), Guam structures (ORNL, HEIGHT empty), French Polynesia BD CARTO PF (Tahiti: 74,849, height and storeys essentially empty). None carries heights.
- Microsoft GlobalML and Google Open Buildings do not cover the Pacific (index read).
- Pacific Data Hub (pacificdata.org) and the SPREP search pages answer a Cloudflare or 403 block to curl and the fetch tool; they were not bypassed (section 10).
- No LiDAR-derived or 3D building model for Suva, Port Moresby or any Pacific capital was found. A Guam post-typhoon 3D integrated mesh exists (scene items, not downloaded).

#### Antarctica
- One row: AADC Our Footprint on Antarctica (CC BY 4.0), pages only. Overture holds 284 OSM buildings in the McMurdo box.

### 5.5 The global datasets

- **Overture (global).** The schema fields, the source list and the monthly cadence are as in row 4.1 of the table; heights are carried per feature with a `sources` entry per property, and what Overture holds is a union of OSM tags (where mappers added them) and the heights Microsoft's model supplied (the sample shows it in Australia, Germany and the Nordics). The Overture docs do not say how any supplier derived its height. The buildings theme as a whole is ODbL-licensed; its sources must be ODbL-compatible, which is why a CC BY set can be added and a CC BY-NC set (GlobalBuildingAtlas polygons and heights, the City of Cape Town layers) cannot.
- **Microsoft GlobalMLBuildingFootprints.** The repository page states CDLA Permissive 2.0 while Overture's attribution page says Microsoft licensed its data to Overture under the ODbL; both are quoted rather than reconciled. The page counts differ internally (999 M in the FAQ, 1.4 B in the intro). Heights are an averaged neural-network estimate per polygon with -1 for none, 174 M footprints with a height after the 2023 update.
- **Google Open Buildings and the 2.5D Temporal set.** v3 has no heights. The 2.5D Temporal set has a height band at about 4 m effective resolution, an accuracy figure (MAE 1.5 m) evaluated only in North America, Europe and Japan, and its coverage list excludes Morocco, Western Sahara and Libya (Africa report). One tile opened (Cape Town 2023; manifest `1d_EPSG_32734_2023_06_30.json`, file `v1/geotiffs/1dcc4_2023_06_30/tile_YXZLSCPCEYo.tif`, 12.5 km x 12.5 km, bands: fractional count, height, presence).
- **GHSL.** GHS-BUILT-H R2023A is a 100 m raster (ANBH) predicted from ALOS, SRTM and Sentinel-2 for 2018; tiles are 1,000 km squares in the Mollweide grid whose origin is x = -18,041,000 m, y = 9,000,000 m (row 3, column 20 holds Berlin; row 14, column 31 Melbourne; row 14, column 20 Cape Town). The JRC page reached through the web fetcher gave no accuracy figure; the Earth Engine page names the methodology paper (DOI 10.1080/17538947.2024.2390454), which was not read. GHS-OBAT puts GHS-BUILT-H's cell mean on 2.28 billion Overture footprints (2.5 m minimum; MAE 2.89 m at feature level per the catalogue page): on the measurements here it would share GHS-BUILT-H's cell-scale errors.
- **WSF 3D.** A 90 m global raster of average height, volume, area and fraction, from TanDEM-X (2012) and the 2019 settlement mask; no figures on its page; not opened.
- **3D-GloBFP.** Per-building heights for 2020 on footprints from Microsoft, OSM, VIDA and EUBUCCO; CC BY 4.0; about 198 GB in ten figshare parts; opened in three tiles (Berlin, Melbourne, Cape Town); it overestimates in the two dense centres.
- **GlobalBuildingAtlas.** Best stated accuracy of the global sets (LoD1 RMSE 1.5 m to 8.9 m by continent, 5.5 m global; Africa not validated), 3 m height rasters, 2.68 billion LoD1 buildings; licence CC BY-NC 4.0 except the ODbL polygon part; the files were not opened because the Hugging Face API requires a login and the mediaTUM record lists rsync and FTP passwords in its text, which are not used. It would also be unmixable with an ODbL base for its heights.
- **OSM.** Simple 3D Buildings defines `height` as the distance from the lowest ground contact to the roof top, excluding antennas and equipment; `building:levels` counts floors above ground without roof levels; `min_height`, `roof:levels`, `roof:shape` and `building:part` complete the schema.
- **Commercial and tile sources.** Google's Photorealistic 3D Tiles terms exclude offline use, bulk download and derived content; Vexcel's 3D City EULA is read (internal use only, paid); Esri and Maxar (now Vantor) are paid and their terms pages could not be read (section 10).

## 6. Nothing better found

### 6.1 Africa: north, west and central

HDX country searches (CKAN package_search through the HDX API, whose address came from the HDX dataset pages returned by the web search; query: building, buildings, footprint, footprints or "3D" within the country group). Each search returned only HOT exports, Google Open Buildings extracts, GRID3 settlement-extent sets, UNOSAT/ITOS event layers, catchment-area sets or World Bank indicator tables:


- Morocco: https://data.humdata.org/api/3/action/package_search?rows=40&fl=name,title,organization&q=%28building%20OR%20buildings%20OR%20footprint%20OR%20footprints%20OR%20%223D%22%29%20AND%20groups%3Amar
- Western Sahara: https://data.humdata.org/api/3/action/package_search?rows=40&fl=name,title,organization&q=%28building%20OR%20buildings%20OR%20footprint%20OR%20footprints%20OR%20%223D%22%29%20AND%20groups%3Aesh
- Algeria: https://data.humdata.org/api/3/action/package_search?rows=40&fl=name,title,organization&q=%28building%20OR%20buildings%20OR%20footprint%20OR%20footprints%20OR%20%223D%22%29%20AND%20groups%3Adza
- Tunisia: https://data.humdata.org/api/3/action/package_search?rows=40&fl=name,title,organization&q=%28building%20OR%20buildings%20OR%20footprint%20OR%20footprints%20OR%20%223D%22%29%20AND%20groups%3Atun
- Libya: https://data.humdata.org/api/3/action/package_search?rows=40&fl=name,title,organization&q=%28building%20OR%20buildings%20OR%20footprint%20OR%20footprints%20OR%20%223D%22%29%20AND%20groups%3Alby
- Egypt: https://data.humdata.org/api/3/action/package_search?rows=40&fl=name,title,organization&q=%28building%20OR%20buildings%20OR%20footprint%20OR%20footprints%20OR%20%223D%22%29%20AND%20groups%3Aegy
- Sudan: https://data.humdata.org/api/3/action/package_search?rows=40&fl=name,title,organization&q=%28building%20OR%20buildings%20OR%20footprint%20OR%20footprints%20OR%20%223D%22%29%20AND%20groups%3Asdn
- Mauritania: https://data.humdata.org/api/3/action/package_search?rows=40&fl=name,title,organization&q=%28building%20OR%20buildings%20OR%20footprint%20OR%20footprints%20OR%20%223D%22%29%20AND%20groups%3Amrt

Portals and pages read, with where the address came from:

- data.gov.ma (CKAN API `/data/api/3/action/package_search`): address from the web search results for Moroccan open data; answers above in A.1.
- data.gov.tn home page https://data.gov.tn/fr/ (address from the data-portal listing returned by the Tunisian search); search pages answered HTTP 500.
- AGIS Abuja https://agis.fcta.gov.ng/agis-at-a-glance/ and OSGOF https://osgof.gov.ng/ (addresses from the Abuja search results): no building product.
- data.gov.gh and geosenegal.gouv.sn (addresses from search results): no connection from this Mac.
- IDECanarias, GRAFCAN blog PDF https://www.grafcan.es/files/20260113_IDECanarias_BlogIDEE.pdf (address from the Canary search results): services only.
- Energy Data (World Bank) CKAN API https://energydata.info/api/3/action/package_search?q=building (address from search results): lists rooftop-solar sets for Accra, Lagos and Lagos State, Liberia digitized structures, DR Congo building points; no other building product for these countries.
- Catastro INSPIRE feeds for the Canary provinces (addresses from the Catastro entry in the web search results; feeds read from Wayback).
- Other units checked and found to have nothing better than a HOT export: every country row above without its own source row (Mauritania, Mali, Niger, Chad, Senegal, Gambia, Guinea-Bissau, Guinea, Sierra Leone, Cote d'Ivoire, Burkina Faso, Togo, Benin, Cameroon, CAR, Equatorial Guinea, Gabon, Congo, Sao Tome and Principe, Cabo Verde, Algeria, Tunisia, Egypt, Western Sahara): the HDX country search and the portals named in their rows.
- Nigerian states Kano, Rivers, Oyo, Kaduna, Ogun, Lagos: one web search for their GIS portals found none; Lagos and Abuja have their own rows. Not each state's site was visited (no state portal address surfaced).

- Zenodo and figshare searches for city building sets with heights or storeys (Lagos, Accra, Kinshasa, Abuja, Dakar, Casablanca, Cairo, Nigeria; Zenodo API queries): only 3D-GloBFP, a Dakar/Nairobi/Dar es Salaam rasterized-footprint population dataset (Zenodo 7139841: raster patches, no heights) and unrelated papers. Figshare's search API answered 200 with empty lists for the city queries.
- Source Cooperative: Open Cities AI Challenge is the only Open Cities set (OSM labels; read in the region tables above).
- Nigerian states, one more route each: Oyo (a news item, via search, says OYOGIS mapped over 45 % of Ibadan; the page answered a Cloudflare block, not read); Kano (KANGIS: news items only; the Kano land-bureau address ksblm.com.ng/about.php from a search result did not connect); Ogun (a Kartoza project page on a state geo-portal, 2012-2014, answered "site inactive" 503); Rivers and FCT (no portal address surfaced beyond AGIS and OSGOF read earlier); Lagos (nairametrics item only). No state publishes buildings.
- National geoportals: the NGDI geoportal is described in search results as online but with no metadata additions since 2010 (GIM International article), no building layer found; OSGOF's open data are administrative boundaries (UN SALB page named in the search results); Nigeria's Federal Ministry of Power runs a GeoNode (electrification, not buildings).
- Maghreb and Egypt: Morocco: GEPMapping solar-cadastre geoportal (gepmapping.ma, address from a news item) did not connect, the Le Matin and Medias24 items answered a Cloudflare challenge; Algeria: a Fadaeldjazair cadastre geoportal (land titles, Domaine national) appears in search results only, no building layer; Tunisia: nothing; Egypt: nothing ().
- Coverage re-check: every country listed in the brief has its own row in the region tables above or in this file. Added here: Ceuta and Melilla (new row), Canary Islands (3D-GloBFP addition), Western Sahara and Libya (replaced rows). Madeira is covered in buildings-europe-w.md (its dados.gov.pt NdD2 row, no buildings in the GeoPackage) and is not repeated. Sao Tome and Principe, Cabo Verde and Equatorial Guinea keep their A.2/A.4 rows (HOT export, GRID3 v3.0, 2.5D) and now also have 3D-GloBFP (world coverage; no box measured). No country is missing.

### 6.2 Africa: east, southern and the islands

Fetched: the portal pages named per line; the portal address source is given in brackets.

- **Western Cape** (https://gis.westerncape.gov.za/server2/rest/services; address from a web search result listing its MapServer layers): root lists 17 folders; SpatialDataWarehouse lists no building service; OpenDataPortal and SG_Data return "Token Required" (not bypassed). Cape Town gets its own rows.
- **Eastern Cape** (https://gis.ecotp.gov.za/; from a search result): no buildings, footprints or LiDAR on the page; 2024 orthophotos only.
- **Gauteng** (https://gcro.ac.za/outputs/dataportal/; from a search result for the Gauteng City-Region Observatory): public infrastructure, land cover and an urban footprint layer; no building outlines. The provincial GIS portal address did not appear in a result, so it is in F.
- **Johannesburg** (https://eservices.joburg.org.za/Sites/cgismaps/Pages/default.aspx, from a search result): own row; LiDAR and orthophotos on request only.
- **National** (https://ngi.dlrrd.gov.za/index.php/what-we-do/maps-and-geospatial-information, from a search result, and the CD:NGI portal it links): no building layer named.
- **Mauritius, Madagascar, Comoros, Seychelles, Zimbabwe, Zambia, Malawi, Mozambique, Angola, Namibia, Botswana, Eswatini, Lesotho, Horn countries:** no national mapping agency portal was found by search; only the HDX and import pages above were read.

- **Tshwane:** ArcGIS server https://e-gis001.tshwane.gov.za/server/rest/services (address from the ArcGIS Hub search "Tshwane building", which returned only Zoning Tshwane layers from this server). Folders read: Other_WS (Contours, Farm_Boundaries, Land_Boundaries, Land_Parcel, Street_Address, Zoning and others), Viewer (Informal_Settlement, Land_Information, Utilities, Zoning_Transparency and others), BaseMaps, Hosted, Housing (empty). Land_Information layers are Sectional Scheme and Multiple Service Point only. No building layer.
- **Buffalo City:** the municipality's GIS Website Manual (https://www.buffalocity.gov.za/CM/uploads/documents/20211205051620804542GISWebsiteManual.pdf, from a search result): 13,946 characters of text read, no mention of buildings, footprints or downloads. Hub search "Buffalo City buildings" returned only Buffalo, New York items.
- **Nelson Mandela Bay:** the viewer address from a search result (gis.nelsonmandelabay.gov.za/AGSViewerCM/, plain http) answers 404; Hub search "Nelson Mandela Bay buildings" returns story maps and a one-feature boundary layer only.
- **Mangaung:** Hub search "Mangaung buildings" returns dashboards and logos from consultants, no building layer.
- **Gauteng province and Johannesburg:** Hub searches "Gauteng buildings" and "Johannesburg building footprints" return property boundary layers (Gauteng Properties, 105 features) and other countries' footprints; none is a Gauteng or Johannesburg building layer.
- **KwaZulu-Natal, Limpopo, Northern Cape, Mpumalanga, Free State, North West:** Hub searches return farm portions (KZN 77,138; Free State 55,535), vegetation and boundary layers only. KZN's COGTA server (giswebmaps1.kzncogta.gov.za) answered HTTP 502 (proxy error) on two calls and Limpopo's agriculture server (gis-server2.agric.limpopo.gov.za) gave no connection, so their catalogues stayed unread. Web searches for Mpumalanga, Free State and North West provincial GIS portals returned no provincial government address (only national or SANBI services).
- **National catalogues:** https://www.eis.africa/nspdr/ (National Spatial Planning Data Repository, 4,548 characters of text) mentions no building data. catalogue.sasdi.gov.za and nspdrsplum.dalrrd.gov.za (addresses from search results) gave no connection. https://opendataza.gitbook.io/toolkit/open-data-resources/land-property-and-infrastructure-data-resources lists no building footprint layer (it mentions only StatsSA building statistics).
- **Addis Ababa, Dar es Salaam, Kampala, Nairobi (heights or storeys):** HDX searches ("Addis Ababa building", "Nairobi building", "Dar es Salaam building", "Kampala building", "Ramani Huria", "Open Cities Africa building") return flood analyses, boundaries and the OSM exports already listed; Zenodo searches return papers and one rasterized-footprint dataset (record 7139841: 0.5 m raster patches of footprints for Dakar, Nairobi and Dar es Salaam, CC BY 4.0, not vector, no height). No set with heights or storeys was found. Zenodo records 16835376 (Pleiades building-height study; ICESat-2 validation shapefile, CC BY-NC) and 22837197 (a 118-place 3D reconstruction that takes heights from Overture and the Google 2.5D raster) are not African city height sets.
- **Row coverage re-check:** every country and territory of the brief has its own row in sections 2.5 to 2.9 (Ethiopia, Eritrea, Djibouti, Somalia with Somaliland, South Sudan, Kenya, Uganda, Rwanda, Burundi, Tanzania with Zanzibar, Malawi, Zambia, Zimbabwe, Mozambique, Angola, Namibia, Botswana, Eswatini, Lesotho, South Africa, Madagascar, Mauritius with Rodrigues, Reunion, Mayotte, Seychelles, Comoros, Saint Helena group, BIOT, French Southern Territories). Nothing missing.

### 6.3 Australia

Each unit below was checked at its own portal or catalogue and has no building product better, newer or more open than the national or state source above. Where the portal address came from is in brackets.

- **City of Sydney (data.cityofsydney.nsw.gov.au).** Its ArcGIS Hub search API answered for the queries building, footprint, height, model, floor, lidar, storey and massing: only floor-space survey blocks, terrain contours, trees and light poles. [Address: council's "Access open data" page in a search result on cityofsydney.nsw.gov.au, then API answered HTTP 200.]
- **Brisbane City Council (data.brisbane.qld.gov.au).** Opendatasoft catalogue search for building (20 hits, all planning overlays, flood, vegetation), height (41 hits, none about structures) and 3D/footprint (0). [Address: link on https://www.brisbane.qld.gov.au/business/tools-and-resources/open-data.] The Queensland state layer covers Brisbane from the 2025 LiDAR.
- **Gold Coast, Moreton Bay, Noosa.** Gold Coast hub: "Building height" (planning designated heights) and "Buildings" (public-land assets); Moreton Bay: "Building Heights Overlay"; Noosa: council-owned buildings. Planning limits and asset points, not measured heights. [Addresses: resource URLs on their data.gov.au records.] The state layer covers all three from LiDAR (Gold Coast 242,183 outlines, 2022).
- **Perth (City of Perth, Landgate).** City of Perth CPS2 maximum-height, setback and plot-ratio plans (planning). Landgate 3D searches returned geology and a Floreat beach mesh. [Address: catalogue.data.wa.gov.au search results.]
- **Darwin and Northern Territory.** data.nt.gov.au (the NT's CKAN, address from the NT portal's About page in a search result) holds no footprint, LiDAR or 3D dataset; City of Darwin's new Data Insights Platform is dashboards ("rather than publishing raw data"); its ArcGIS hub is private (401).
- **Canberra (ACTmapi hub).** Searches for 3D, building height and footprints gave canopy cover and footprints only; the footprint layer has no height. [Address: resource URLs on data.gov.au.]
- **Hobart.** Footprints with year of construction; "3D Hobart" returned nothing. 
- **Newcastle, Wollongong.** data.gov.au searches returned NSW Spatial Services railway and road segment 3D files and Wollongong heritage-site LiDAR scans; no council building layer. Geoscape's March 2026 release lists updates for Newcastle City Council (119 km2) and Wollongong City Council (162 km2) (closed).
- **Digital Atlas of Australia (digital.atlas.gov.au).** Hub search found ABS building approvals and Built Up Areas only. [Address: linked from a ga.gov.au page.]
- **Adelaide City Council** is in A3; **City of Salisbury** Building Heights (2014) zip is gone (HTTP 404).

- **Western Australia (Landgate/SLIP, DataWA):** read the DataWA CKAN records for DPIRD-084, LGATE-496, LGATE-105 and LGATE-139 and Landgate's licensing page (row above). Perth: the City of Perth's 64 DataWA records include only CPS2 height-limit, setback and plot-ratio plans; a 3D search on DataWA found geology and a Floreat beach mesh. Nothing better than the first report.
- **Victoria:** data.vic.gov.au (CKAN at discover.data.vic.gov.au): searches for Vicmap buildings, Digital Twin Victoria, building height and 3D building return Vicmap Features building polygons/points (CC BY), the DTV platform record (CC BY, a link to the viewer) and the City of Melbourne sets; no Vicmap Buildings download record. The live land.vic.gov.au Vicmap pages answer a Cloudflare challenge (403) and were not used; the licence reading comes from the DTV portal's own catalogue JSON (row above).
- **South Australia (location.sa.gov.au / data.sa.gov.au):** location.sa.gov.au answers a CloudFront 403 from here and was not used. data.sa.gov.au (CKAN): footprint, building outlines (0), LiDAR, 3D model, building heights, building footprints return the DEW urban canopy and built-environment sets (raster), the City of Adelaide 3D model, planning height limits and the Salisbury heights (zip gone, 404). Nothing better.
- **Northern Territory:** data.nt.gov.au (CKAN) returned one building-related record (Darwin Harbour habitat mapping) and none for footprint, LiDAR or 3D beyond unrelated hits; City of Darwin's Data Insights Platform is dashboards and its ArcGIS hub is private. Nothing found.
- **Australian Capital Territory:** data.act.gov.au's Socrata catalogue (177 hits for building: permits, registers and asset layers; 0 for 3D; 3 for footprint, all links to canopy or class-B units; 2 for LiDAR, links to canopy cover and vegetation map) plus the ACTmapi footprint FeatureServer (64,672, no height, static). Nothing better.
- **Tasmania:** the LIST service and the data.gov.au record (row above), Hobart's hub (footprints only; searches for 3D and lidar returned nothing) and Launceston. Nothing better.
- **City of Sydney:** Data Hub search returned no building layer. A search snippet says the council's 3D massing model comes from AAM Group under a licence that does not allow sharing; its source page (a Right to Know reply) answered a Cloudflare challenge here, so this is unverified. Not downloadable.
- **Brisbane City Council (3D):** the page at brisbane.qld.gov.au/planning-building/planning-guidelines-tools/online-tools/virtual-brisbane redirected to a general planning-tools list with no Virtual Brisbane text, download or licence; the Opendatasoft catalogue has no 3D or footprint dataset. Not downloadable; the Queensland state LiDAR outlines cover Brisbane.
- **Perth, Hobart, Canberra, Darwin city models:** no downloadable city 3D model found at their portals (Perth: DataWA search above; Hobart hub: footprints only; Canberra: ACT catalogue above, a 2015 LiDAR listing points to ELVIS, not fetched; Darwin: nothing). The only downloadable city 3D models found in Australia are Adelaide (CC BY, 3DS), Melbourne (mesh, cloud, footprints; CC BY) and Geelong (massing KMZ, roofprints, CC BY).

Fetched: the pages named.

- Wellington City open-data Hub feed (https://data-wcc.opendata.arcgis.com/api/feed/dcat-us/1.1.json): 64 3D-building items, heritage and district-plan layers; no height attribute and no footprint dataset in the Hub list.
- Auckland Council Hub feed (https://data-aucklandcouncil.opendata.arcgis.com/api/feed/dcat-us/1.1.json): 235 datasets; no building footprint or height dataset other than the DSM and the visualization-only CBD scene; its 'Footprint' item is an aerial survey flight footprint.
- French Polynesia, Te Fenua (https://www.tefenua.gov.pf/): the page is a script application with no readable catalogue or API; no further building set found there; BD CARTO PF (data.gouv.fr) stays the French Polynesia source.
- New Caledonia, Georep (https://georep.nc/) and data.gouv.nc: see the replacement row.
- HDX (https://data.humdata.org/api/3/action/package_search?q=Solomon+Islands+building+footprints&rows=10 ; https://data.humdata.org/api/3/action/package_search?q=Kiribati+building+footprints+Tarawa&rows=10 ; https://data.humdata.org/api/3/action/package_search?q=building+height+Tonga+OR+Vanuatu+OR+Fiji+OR+Samoa&rows=10 ; https://data.humdata.org/api/3/action/package_search?q=drone+buildings+Pacific&rows=10): no HOT, UNOSAT or drone building set with heights for Papua New Guinea, Fiji, Samoa, Tonga, Vanuatu, Solomon Islands, Kiribati or Tuvalu; only OSM exports and UNOSAT damage assessments (CC BY-SA).
- Per-country geoportals of Papua New Guinea, Fiji, Samoa, Tonga, Vanuatu, Solomon Islands, Kiribati, Tuvalu, Nauru, Marshall Islands: no portal address was found on any fetched page, so none was visited (see Unverified).
- Territory coverage: Cook Islands, Niue, Tokelau, Papua New Guinea, Fiji, Solomon Islands, Vanuatu, New Caledonia, French Polynesia, Wallis and Futuna, Samoa, American Samoa, Tonga, Tuvalu, Kiribati, Nauru, Marshall Islands, FSM, Palau, Guam, Northern Mariana Islands, Pitcairn and Antarctica have rows in sections 3.5 to 3.7; South Georgia, Bouvet, Chatham and Norfolk are in the new row above.

### 7.3 Australia

Fetched: OSM wiki import pages and the data.gov.au mirrors.

- **OpenStreetMap.** OSM buildings in Australia are the base of Overture's footprints in Brisbane (87 % OSM), Melbourne (98 %), Ballarat (79 %) and Geelong (97 %) boxes. Heights in OSM come partly from council imports: the Geelong roofprints (plan 2014, CC BY 3.0 with data.gov.au explicit permission, "ODbL compliance verified: yes") and the Ballarat 3D buildings (draft plan, ODbL compliance blank). These are the reason Overture already has council heights there.
- **Microsoft ML Buildings** supply most Overture heights in Gold Coast (73 % of footprints), Brisbane fringes, Ballarat and Launceston; they are the ones the QLD ridge heights run 1.6 to 1.8 times above in median.
- **AURIN** redistributes the Geoscape SA2 samples (login-gated).
- No academic or NGO national Australian building-height set was found beyond the global ones (not repeated here: Microsoft, Google, GHSL, GBA, 3D-GloBFP, WSF3D, Esri, Cesium, Google 3D Tiles as in the other reports).
- **Commercial:** Geoscape (A1). Metromap and similar photogrammetry vendors appeared in search results (not fetched), commercial only.

### 7.4 New Zealand, the Pacific and Antarctica

Fetched: the HDX package search, the Microsoft tile index, the Google Open Buildings page, ArcGIS Online search for third-party items.

- HOT OSM country exports on HDX (ODbL; one per Pacific country; the same data Overture carries): see the region tables.
- Esri's "Auckland, New Zealand Buildings" 3D scene (owner esri_3d) and Esri's Fiji settlement points: seen in ArcGIS Online search results only, Esri-licensed content, not examined.
- UNOSAT damage assessments (Fiji 2016, Vanuatu 2020): event-specific damage points, not a building layer.
- SPREP country portals host OSM extracts for Guam under the SPREP Public Licence (record pages read): https://pacific-data.sprep.org/dataset/218 ; https://vanuatu-data.sprep.org/dataset/287
- No HOT/Open Cities, GRID3 or drone-mapped building set with heights was found for the Pacific; PCRAFI exposure layers (building value layers for Samoa, Vanuatu, Tonga, Tuvalu) appeared only in search results of the Pacific Data Hub, which is blocked (section 10).

## 8. The Overture sample

Overture release 2026-10-21.0 (the newest of 2026-09-23.0, 2026-09-23.1 and 2026-10-21.0 on the bucket), buildings type: 512 Parquet parts, 276.9 GB (S3 listing summed). Method: pyarrow's anonymous S3 filesystem; the footers of all parts were read once to build a row-group index by bounding-box statistics; per box only the overlapping row groups were read (columns id, bbox, height, num_floors, sources, geometry) and buildings whose bbox lies inside the box kept. Boxes are 0.05 degrees. "Height supplied by" is the dataset named by the feature's `/properties/height` source entry; where a feature has a height but no such entry, the feature's own first source dataset is used (OpenStreetMap record or Microsoft ML record). The Europe, Asia, Americas and USA reports sampled release 2026-09-23.1, so their shares are not directly comparable box for box.

(1) The suppliers named in these 30 boxes: OpenStreetMap (every box), Microsoft ML Buildings (Australian city boxes 1.2 % (Hobart) to 41.5 % (Darwin) of footprints, rural NSW 91.4 %, Tunis 10,250 of 19,282, Luanda 17,477 of 41,258, Cairo 7,855 of 20,749; and the heights across Australia), Google Open Buildings (the African boxes: Kinshasa 40,571 of 49,027, Addis Ababa 47,116 of 56,637, Luanda 21,941 of 41,258) and Esri Community Maps (New Zealand: Auckland 1,220, Wellington 388, Christchurch 1,121; rural Canterbury 336 of 634). No national or city source appears: not LINZ, not City of Melbourne, not Queensland. (2) Heights: Microsoft ML supplies the heights in every Australian box (93.6 % of Sydney's, 61.4 % of Melbourne's, 97.4 % of Brisbane's, 99.8 % of Hobart's and 99.4 % of Darwin's height values, 99.99 % of rural NSW's); OSM record heights are all there are in New Zealand, Fiji, Papua New Guinea and every African box. (3) Storeys are OSM tags: 64.9 % in Sydney, 66.0 % in Dar es Salaam (an OSM storey effort), 26.1 % in Wellington, 26.5 % in Cape Town; 0.1 % in Addis Ababa. (4) Rural boxes: NSW 90.0 % height from Microsoft ML; rural Kenya 99.9 % and rural Canterbury 100.0 % with neither. (5) Africa: the buildings with neither height nor storeys run from 34.0 % (Dar es Salaam) to 99.6 % (Dakar, Luanda); Australia from 10.0 % (rural NSW) to 80.3 % (Melbourne); Pacific: Suva 98.0 %.


## 9. Overlap with Overture (what each source would add)

### 9.1 Africa: north, west and central

Files opened: the Google Open Buildings 2.5D Temporal GeoTIFF tiles (2023 band `building_height`), read by HTTP window from the Google bucket for each box through the tile index in its JSON manifests. Overture release 2026-10-21.0 footprints in each 0.05 degree box (dump through ovbox.py). Method: each Overture footprint (centroid in the box) was rasterized onto the 2.5D grid (0.5 m pixels averaged 2 x 2 to 1 m, `all_touched`), and the mean of `building_height` over its pixels was taken; negative values (no-data) are dropped. The 2.5D raster has no footprints of its own, so "matched" is by construction: the "2.5D raster values on" column is the share of Overture buildings with at least one valid pixel. A building "gets" a height when its mean is 2 m or more (an arbitrary cut to separate roofs from ground noise; P10/P50/P90/P99 are given so any other cut can be read). Heights are metres, estimated per pixel from Sentinel-2 (about 4 m effective resolution), not roof-max minus ground from a survey. Overture's own heights here are OSM `height` tags (the record's own source); its storeys are OSM `building:levels`. Comparisons with Overture heights are small samples except Bamako, Abuja, Tunis and Cairo.

Reading the table: in every African box the 2.5D raster would give most buildings their first height, but it is a coarse estimate (median 3.5 to 5 m in West and Central African boxes, 8.0 m in Cairo), with partial raster coverage in Kinshasa (73 %), Tunis (52 %), Abuja (69 %), Kano (77 %), Khartoum (50 %), Freetown (39 %) and Libreville (92 %), probably water, no-data or missing tiles (not diagnosed). The OSM-tag comparison is lower and spreads widely (Lagos median ratio 0.67 on 66 buildings; Abuja 0.59 on 239; Tunis 0.71 on 160; Bamako 0.94 on 1,273 with 37 % within 20 %; Cairo 0.99 on 89, 47 % within 20 %). The OSM `height` tags at Abidjan (n=14) are implausible (median difference 34 m) and the Khartoum sample (n=24, ratio 0.36) is small. No source file from the Canary Islands, Morocco or the Nigerian state portals was opened.

**3D-GloBFP against Overture, 13 African boxes.**

Files opened: 3D-GloBFP shapefiles (attribute `Height`, metres, year 2020), grids listed in the table, from figshare (no login). Method: source building = a 3D-GloBFP polygon whose centroid lies in the box; matched = its centroid lies inside an Overture footprint (centroid-in-polygon); where several sources fall in one Overture polygon their heights are averaged. "Gain" = Overture buildings with neither height nor storeys that receive a matched source height. Heights are the file's value (an ML estimate of building height in metres, 2020), not a roof-minus-ground survey; Overture's own heights are OSM tags (Microsoft ML in the Canary Islands).

Reading it: 3D-GloBFP matches 59 to 77 % of its buildings to an Overture footprint in most boxes (98 % in Bamako, where Overture is all OSM; 74 % in Las Palmas but with only a third as many buildings as Overture). It gives a height to most buildings that have neither (Bamako 99 %, Kinshasa 96 %, Abuja 79 %, Tunis 80 %, Accra 65 %, Lagos 57 %), which is better than the 2.5D raster in Kinshasa, Abuja and Tunis (raster gaps) and similar elsewhere. Its heights are higher than OSM tags in Algiers (median ratio 2.2, n=16), Tripoli and Las Palmas, near 1 in Accra, Abuja, Cairo, Bamako (1.24) and Tunis (0.92), lower in Kinshasa and Khartoum (0.4). Within-20 % shares are 1 to 57 %. Against 3 m x storeys the median ratio is 0.6 to 2.3 and the correlation 0.15 to 0.62, so it is a rough guide only.

### 9.2 Africa: east, southern and the islands

Fetched: real files or live service answers, each opened and measured against Overture release 2026-10-21.0 with ovbox.py. Method: a source building counts as matched when its representative point falls inside an Overture footprint, or failing that when the two overlap by more than half of the smaller one; "source gain" = matched source buildings that carry a height while the Overture building has neither height nor storeys, as a share of all source buildings; "Overture gain" = such Overture buildings as a share of all Overture buildings in the box; height comparison uses matched pairs where both have a height (metres). Source boxes are by intersection, Overture by full containment, so source counts at the box edge run slightly high.

Reading: where both have heights the numbers rest on few pairs in Cape Town and Kigali (27, 40, 17), so they are indicative only; Mayotte (2,022 pairs) is solid and shows BD TOPO taller than the OSM heights Overture holds (ratio 1.8, only 14 % within 20 %). In every box Overture's footprints are mostly OSM (Reunion 20,449 of 24,995; Mayotte 18,884 of 19,993; Cape Town 1,828 of 1,948; Kigali 75,860 of 112,464). The Cape Town low-detail layer merges some CBD blocks, which explains the low pair agreement. Johannesburg, Nairobi, Addis Ababa, Dar es Salaam and Luanda were not measured: no openable building file was reached (Johannesburg LiDAR on request; the energydata zips behind a Cloudflare challenge; Luanda nothing found).

The source has 2.5 times more footprints than Overture here; 1,421 of them (18 %) have no Overture footprint, so it could add outlines (not heights), subject to a licence that is not stated.

### 9.3 Australia

Fetched: each source's real data (queries, zip or shapefile) and Overture 2026-10-21.0 buildings in a 0.05 degree box through shared/ovbox.py. Matching: a source building is matched to an Overture footprint if its centroid lies in an Overture polygon, or the two overlap by more than 50 % of the smaller area (best overlap kept). Gain = matched source building with a height where the matched Overture building has none; the "Overture buildings gaining" column counts distinct Overture buildings. Pairs = matched buildings where both have a height. Differences are source minus Overture. Units are metres. Meaning of the source height: QLD = roof maximum (bsm_max) or eave (bsm_edge) elevation minus ground elevation at the centroid, from LiDAR; Melbourne = structure_extrusion (structure's top elevation minus its lowest elevation); Ballarat = roof minus ground elevation; Geelong ROOF_HT = approximate roof height; Launceston Height_m = per-survey height; Christmas and Cocos = mean DSM minus mean DEM over the polygon (2011).

Reading: Queensland is the strongest state-scale gain (a quarter of Overture buildings in Brisbane's box and 18 % on the Gold Coast get a measured height; the ridge heights run 1.6 to 1.8 times Overture's median, the eave heights agree). City of Melbourne adds a height to 38 % of the CBD box's Overture buildings and its medians agree (0.95). Launceston adds a height to two thirds of Overture's buildings in its box (a great many sheds and garages). Geelong adds almost nothing because Overture already carries its heights (OSM import). Overture's own height share in these boxes: Brisbane 66 %, Gold Coast 78 %, Melbourne 17 %, Ballarat 44 %, Geelong 90 %, Launceston 15 %, Christmas Island 38 %, Cocos 0.5 %. Footprint sources in the Overture boxes: Brisbane 17,517 OSM and 2,687 Microsoft; Gold Coast 10,567 Microsoft and 3,930 OSM; Melbourne 22,836 OSM and 369 Microsoft; Norfolk Island box 3,035 OSM, none with height or floors. Overture num_floors in the Melbourne box 22.5 % (5,228 of 23,205), Brisbane 29.3 %, Gold Coast 6.8 %.

**City of Adelaide 3D model.**

Fetched: the City of Adelaide untextured block zip (opened) and Overture 2026-10-21.0 buildings in the box 138.56,-34.96 to 138.65,-34.89 (54,902 buildings, 35,170 with height; 36,267 OSM footprints, 18,635 Microsoft). Method: parse every .3ds vertex (2.61 M after dropping point objects), register the model to Overture by FFT cross-correlation of occupied 4 m cells (offset about 278,436 E, 6,130,514 N in MGA54; 1 m refinement found no better shift), then for each Overture building take the vertices inside its footprint and use the 99th minus 1st percentile Z as height (metres; roof-top minus lowest model point under the footprint, so it includes any podium or terrain relief). Results: 6,465 Overture buildings had enough model vertices; 4,595 of them (71.1 %) have no Overture height and would gain one; 1,859 pairs with both heights: median absolute difference 2.91 m, median signed (model minus Overture) +2.68 m, median ratio 1.51, 23.1 % within 20 %. Treat as indicative: the model has no stated CRS, was registered by pattern matching, and meshes are not separated per building.

### 9.4 New Zealand, the Pacific and Antarctica

Fetched: source files opened (REST queries returning the records of each box) and Overture release 2026-10-21.0 through the shared helper ovbox.py. Matching: the source polygon's representative point inside an Overture footprint; the NZ LINZ, WCC, CCC, Tauranga, Queenstown and Palau/Guam/Samoa runs also accept a source polygon more than 50 % covered by one Overture footprint; the Dunedin storeys comparison and the Papeete run use the point-in-polygon test only. "Gain" counts source buildings with a height (or storeys) that sit on an Overture building with none; "Overture gain" counts distinct Overture buildings that would receive one. Heights are metres; differences are source minus Overture.

Suva and Port Moresby: no openable official footprint or height file was found, so no overlap was measured; Overture itself holds 8,285 (Suva box) and 3,073 (Port Moresby box) OSM buildings. Tauranga's box and the Wellington box hold urban rather than suburban areas, so shares are not national figures. Wellington's and Tauranga's heights are source attributes whose definition the publishers do not state.

**Wellington City footprints, re-measured.**

Re-measured: Wellington box (174.770,-41.295,174.790,-41.275) with Overture 2026-10-21.0: 2,892 WCC outlines, 2,160 (74.7 %) in an Overture footprint, 1,994 of 2,446 Overture buildings (81.5 %) matched. Of 30 pairs where Overture has a height, approx_hei minus Overture height: median abs 7.3 m, median signed -5.5 m, 43 % within 20 %; approx_hei minus base_elev gives median abs 10.2 m, 37 % within 20 %. Both are elevation-like values, so no height gain is claimed. The Auckland and Wellington 3D models are not tabular and were not measured; no openable file exists for Suva or Port Moresby.

### 9.5 Global height sets against trusted heights (four boxes)

Fetched: the trusted source files (the Berlin LoD2 tile https://gdi.berlin.de/data/a_lod2/atom/LoD2_392_5819.zip; City of Melbourne's 2023 footprints https://data.melbourne.vic.gov.au/api/explore/v2.1/catalog/datasets/2023-building-footprints/exports/geojson; the City of Cape Town's photogrammetric layer https://citymaps.capetown.gov.za/agsext/rest/services/Theme_Based/ODP_SPLIT_6/MapServer/2; NYC's building footprints https://data.cityofnewyork.us/resource/5zhs-2jue.geojson), the GHS-BUILT-H tiles, the 3D-GloBFP grid files, one Google 2.5D Temporal tile, and one GlobalBuildingAtlas tile from the Zenodo record 18361667 (a Europe-only, 0.1 degree re-tiling of GBA by a University of Trier author, CC BY-NC 4.0, anonymous download; the tile e01340_n5260_e01350_n5250 of GBA_tiles.de.tar.xz, 2,180,318,696 bytes), all opened. Method: each trusted building with a height of 2 m or more is represented by an interior point; the product's value is the Overture polygon containing the point (height, with its supplier), the 3D-GloBFP or GBA polygon containing it, the GHS-BUILT-H 100 m cell containing it, or the mean of the 2.5D height pixels under the footprint. Differences are product minus trusted, in metres. Trusted heights: Berlin LoD2 `measuredHeight` (544 buildings, median 16.0 m; tile 392_5819, box 13.4087..13.4229 x 52.5105..52.5195); City of Melbourne `structure_extrusion` (10,763 structures, median 7.0 m, one polygon per structure; box 144.94..144.99 x -37.83..-37.79; most structures are low, so ratios are sensitive); City of Cape Town `BLD_HGT` (4,291 footprints, median 8.9 m; box 18.405..18.435 x -33.935..-33.915; photogrammetric, non-commercial licence); NYC `height_roof` in feet converted to metres (6,483 buildings, median 18.9 m; Midtown box -74.00..-73.97 x 40.74..40.77). Overture matches: Berlin 230 of 544 trusted buildings fall in an Overture polygon, Melbourne 9,800 of 10,763, Cape Town 3,545 of 4,291, NYC 6,390 of 6,483. Overture's NYC heights are OSM tags imported from this same NYC layer, so that pair agrees by construction. Google's 2.5D raster does not cover Berlin, Melbourne or NYC, and the GBA tile re-tiling covers Europe only.

| Box (trusted buildings) | Product | Pairs | Median abs diff (m) | Median signed diff (m) | Median ratio | Within 20 % | RMSE (m) | Correlation |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|

| Central Cape Town (4,291) | GHS-BUILT-H against the mean trusted height of the cell (294 cells) | 294 | 3.74 | +3.49 | 1.35 | 31.6 % | 5.98 | 0.77 |
| Central Cape Town (4,291) | Google 2.5D Temporal 2023, mean over the footprint (the tile covers only part of the box) | 909 | 2.24 | -1.72 | 0.80 | 38.6 % | 8.52 | 0.84 |
| Midtown Manhattan (6,483) | Overture height, all suppliers (OSM import of the same layer) | 6,329 (97.6 %) | 0.03 | 0.00 | 1.00 | 93.9 % | 18.39 | 0.88 |
