| Midtown Manhattan (6,483) | 3D-GloBFP | 6,307 (97.3 %) | 44.70 | +43.69 | 3.12 | 6.8 % | 66.02 | 0.39 |
| Midtown Manhattan (6,483) | GHS-BUILT-H cell value at the building | 6,483 | 12.81 | +4.94 | 1.27 | 16.7 % | 37.97 | 0.20 |
| Midtown Manhattan (6,483) | GHS-BUILT-H against the mean trusted height of the cell (477 cells) | 477 | 8.42 | -2.86 | 0.90 | 34.8 % | 20.74 | 0.33 |

Reading. (1) Overture's Microsoft-supplied heights are the closest building-level values where they exist (Berlin ratio 0.84, 64.4 % within 20 %; Melbourne 0.88), but they exist on 13.5 % to 17.1 % of the trusted buildings in Melbourne and Berlin, on under 1 % in Cape Town and on 51 of 6,483 in Midtown (where its error is large: median -37.0 m); its OSM-supplied values are accurate only where an import ran (Midtown) and noisy elsewhere (RMSE 45.7 m in Melbourne, 48.2 m in Cape Town). (2) 3D-GloBFP covers 58 % to 97 % of the trusted buildings but its error is not random: in the three dense centres it runs well above the trusted heights (median +8.29 m in Berlin Mitte, +6.41 m in Melbourne, +43.69 m in Midtown, where its 668 polygons in the box have a median area of about 7,000 m2 (merged blocks, median height 70.6 m) against 6,483 trusted buildings); only 6.8 % to 26.4 % are within 20 %; in Cape Town it has no bias (+0.43 m) and 28.4 % within 20 %. (3) The GlobalBuildingAtlas tile (Berlin only) runs 3.08 m low (ratio 0.84, correlation 0.60, 19.9 % within 20 %): closer than 3D-GloBFP in Berlin Mitte (median absolute 6.75 m against 9.33 m) but not better than Overture's Microsoft heights; it was not measurable in Melbourne, Cape Town or New York (the anonymous mirror covers Europe only). (4) GHS-BUILT-H is useful only as a cell average: per cell in Cape Town (294 cells) correlation 0.77 with a +3.49 m bias; in Melbourne it runs about twice the trusted mean. (5) Google's 2.5D raster is the best of the global rasters where it exists: lowest median absolute difference (2.24 m) and highest correlation (0.84) in Cape Town; it does not cover Europe, Australia, New Zealand or North America. The 13-box African comparison against OSM heights and 3 m x storeys is in section 9.1. (6) The trusted heights have their own definitions (Berlin: roof maximum minus lowest ground; Melbourne: structure maximum minus minimum elevation; Cape Town: photogrammetric roof height; NYC: roof height from lidar), so differences of about 1 m are inside the trusted sources' own noise; each is a single box, not a national validation.

## 10. Unverified (routes tried are short)

### 10.1 Africa: north, west and central

- Catastro Canary zips (www.catastro.hacienda.gob.es): TLS verification fails on this Mac and on the Mac mini (certificate chain), so no zip was downloaded and the host was not accessed with verification off. Feeds were read from the Wayback copy.
- energydata.info file downloads (Lagos, Lagos State, Accra rooftop footprints, Liberia structures): answered a Cloudflare challenge page; not bypassed. API records read.
- gfdrr.org (Open Cities Africa final report): HTTP 403; read from the Wayback copy instead. Columbia Academic Commons (GRID3 v1 datasets): bot-protection page.
- data.gov.gh, geosenegal.gouv.sn: no connection. data.gov.tn search pages: HTTP 500. Egyptian Survey Authority, Tunisian OTC, ANCFCC geoportal, BNETD/CCT, Lagos LASG, Nigerian state GIS agencies, Algerian agencies: no site located by search that could be opened.
- Kano building-detection dataset on Figshare, Colouring Ghana, the OSGOF Abuja 3D city view, Freetown community-drone data, Togo's national geoportal: seen only as search-result titles or news items; not fetched.
- Which GRID3 country v3.0 settlement sets carry building counts and any height field beyond v4 was not read (only the NGA v4.1 and COD v4.0 release notes and the Mali v3.0 HDX record).

- energydata.info file downloads (Lagos, Lagos State, Accra rooftop footprints, Liberia structures) stay behind a Cloudflare challenge; Zenodo, HDX, figshare and Source Cooperative mirrors of them were searched and none found.
- Oyo and Kano state pages (Cloudflare block, no connection), Moroccan geoportal items (no connection / Cloudflare), Catastro zips for the Canary Islands, Ceuta and Melilla (certificate chain does not verify; nothing downloaded), 3D-GloBFP grids for Ceuta, Niamey, Freetown, Conakry (listed, not downloaded).
- 3D-GloBFP heights were compared only with OSM and Microsoft heights; no surveyed reference exists for these boxes.

### 10.2 Africa: east, southern and the islands

Fetched: nothing for these; routes tried are named.

- Tshwane, Nelson Mandela Bay, Buffalo City, Mangaung: web searches found no metro building layer or portal address (a Tshwane e-GIS viewer and a Nelson Mandela Bay viewer are named in search results only).
- KwaZulu-Natal (giswebmaps1.kzncogta.gov.za), Limpopo (gis-server2.agric.limpopo.gov.za), Northern Cape planning system, Mpumalanga, Free State, North West, and the Gauteng provincial GIS portal: addresses came from search results; the two REST roots I called returned no JSON, the rest were not reached or not found.
- CD:NGI building layer and terms: portal loads by script; the page I read names no layers.
- Energydata.info rooftop-solar downloads (Addis Ababa, Nairobi, Dar es Salaam, Johannesburg and other SA towns): Cloudflare challenge on the download URL; not bypassed; the Wayback index showed a capture only of the small Addis CSV, which was not fetched.
- Saint Helena government footprints (SAERI data portal listing): the page answered HTTP 500.
- IGN BD TOPO quality report for Reunion (RCQ 974 PDF): the URL returned an HTML app shell, not the PDF; the hauteur definition (eave or ridge) is therefore unread.
- WorldPop buildings v2.0 README: PDF text not readable through the fetch tool; Ecopia/Digitize Africa records (Columbia) not fetched.
- Country authorities (Ethiopia, Kenya, Uganda, Tanzania, Rwanda, Mozambique, Angola, Namibia, Botswana, Zimbabwe, Zambia, Malawi, Madagascar, Mauritius, Seychelles, Eswatini, Lesotho): no official agency page with a buildings product was fetched; searches pointed to none.

- KwaZulu-Natal and Limpopo provincial servers (HTTP 502 and no connection); Mpumalanga, Free State, North West, Northern Cape provincial portals (none found); catalogue.sasdi.gov.za and nspdrsplum.dalrrd.gov.za (no connection).
- Mangaung, Nelson Mandela Bay: no live portal found.
- Nairobi 3D scene mesh: heights not derived; origin of the mesh unknown.
- Johannesburg open file: none reachable; its LiDAR remains on request.

### 10.3 Australia

- Geoscape Buildings files and the March 2026 data: closed, nothing opened; accuracy statements are the publisher's.
- NSW 3D Buildings (LoD 2.0): named on one Strata 3D record, no dataset or licence found; the Digital Twin service root answered MissingKey (signed access, not pursued).
- Vicmap Buildings: the live page is behind a Cloudflare challenge (403, not bypassed); the quoted late-2025 text came from an Internet Archive capture whose date I did not pin (a request for 2026-07-31 redirected to December 2025, and a later retry was refused by the permission system, so I stopped); licence and price not published on that page.
- data.qld.gov.au dataset pages (WAF challenge) and gisservices.qra.qld.gov.au (403): the series description came via data.gov.au instead. Queensland's `Building outlines [generated]` per-feature licence is the item info's "no use limitations"; the data.gov.au series licence is CC BY 4.0.
- AURIN downloads for the Geoscape SA2 samples (login-gated, not opened).
- Landgate deep-learning outlines and fee-based topo buildings: access-limited, not opened. Western Australia's DPIRD set: service queried, no file downloaded.
- Adelaide's 3DS models, Yarra's 3DS files, and the Manningham, Shepparton and Wyndham footprints: described from catalogue pages and a zip listing only; attributes unread. The City of Adelaide 2 GB textured zip was not downloaded.
- Melbourne mesh and point-cloud sizes, and CLUE storeys joined to footprints: not measured.
- Antarctic Data Centre: its record search API ignored the query text (3,435 records returned for every query), so station building data for the Australian Antarctic Territory was not found, not ruled out. Heard Island and McDonald Islands: data.gov.au search returned expedition reports only.
- Salisbury's Building Heights zip: HTTP 404.

- Adelaide's origin workbooks (.xls) were not parsed; the registration is inferred (above).
- NSW layer-level metadata for Cowra_building and Strata3D (no JSON from the layer endpoints; I3S service roots anonymous only). Nothing downloaded.
- Right to Know reply on the City of Sydney model (Cloudflare challenge) and land.vic.gov.au pages (403): not read; not bypassed.
- Virtual Brisbane: page redirected to a general list.
- ELVIS (Canberra 2015 LiDAR; Victorian regional town 3D data): not fetched.
- DTV tile sets were not downloaded (viewing terms; cost-recovery licensing).

### 10.4 New Zealand, the Pacific and Antarctica

Fetched or attempted: the blocked catalogue and portal hosts named here (no page content of them was used).

- data.govt.nz (national catalogue and its CKAN API): bot-check page ("Pardon Our Interruption") to curl and to the fetch tool; routes tried: the CKAN API, a dataset page, ArcGIS Online items instead. Licences for WCC footprints and Auckland data are therefore unconfirmed.
- Pacific Data Hub (pacificdata.org): Cloudflare challenge to the API and a 403 to the fetch tool; its dashboard host dashboard.pacificdata.org returned 403; the UAT mirror was unreachable (connection refused); Wayback CDX returned nothing for the API or dataset paths. PCRAFI/PARTneR-2 building value layers (Samoa, Vanuatu, Tonga, Tuvalu, Cook Islands, Solomon Islands) are therefore unexamined.
- SPREP portal search pages (pacific-data.sprep.org and the country sub-portals): 403 to curl; individual dataset pages work. Other SPREP building sets (if any) were not listed.
- New Caledonia: data.gouv.nc API query for building datasets returned no result structure; Georep BDTOPO-NC building content unconfirmed (a bulletin describes the database only).
- Guam BSP hub footprint layer itself (distinct from the ORNL structures layer) was not located as a service.
- Wellington regional server mapping.gw.govt.nz and Marlborough's gis.marlborough.govt.nz: TLS certificate not verified on this Mac; not retried with checks off.
- Antarctica AADC download (data.aad.gov.au, JavaScript page): file not obtained.
- Fiji, Solomon Islands, Vanuatu, Tonga, Kiribati, Tuvalu, Nauru, Wallis and Futuna, Pitcairn national surveys or ministries: no official building set found through HDX, ArcGIS Online and web search; no ministry portals fetched.

- data.govt.nz stays blocked; one Wayback id_ capture of its Wellington record (2019) answers, others have no capture.
- Pacific Data Hub (pacificdata.org): still challenged; an alternate (UAT) host seen in search results was not used (a request to it was refused by the permission system, so not tried).
- Te Fenua catalogue, Georep download platform and BDTOPO-NC building content: script pages, nothing readable.
- National geoportals of PNG, Fiji, Samoa, Tonga, Vanuatu, Solomon Islands, Kiribati, Tuvalu: no address on any fetched page.

### 10.5 The global datasets

- GlobalBuildingAtlas files outside Europe: the Hugging Face dataset API answered HTTP 401 "Invalid username or password"; the mediaTUM record https://mediatum.ub.tum.de/1782307 offers rsync and FTP access with passwords printed in the record's text; no login was made and the printed passwords were not used. Routes tried: the GitHub README (no sizes), the Earth Engine community catalogue page (polygons only), Zenodo (Europe tiles, CC BY-NC 4.0; not downloaded).
- WSF 3D tiles: the DLR page was read; the download service was not fetched.
- GHS-BUILT-H accuracy: the pages read (human-settlement.emergency.copernicus.eu, Earth Engine) give none; the methodology paper was not read. GHS-BUILT-V and GHS-OBAT: Earth Engine catalogue pages only; the JRC data catalogue entries were not opened.
- EUBUCCO's licence: not on its home page (https://eubucco.com/); the Europe reports hold it.
- Esri and Maxar terms: the Esri developer terms page lists topics only (caching, offline use, storing and exporting data, 3D buildings, derivative works) without the text; Esri's 3D Buildings blog answered 403; Maxar's legal page redirects to vantor.com, whose group-licence page shows only headings (internal use, copying and storing, derivative products, redistribution, term). Their terms stay unread; nothing here relies on them beyond "paid".
- Microsoft's 2026 height additions (Vexcel-derived) appear only in a search-result summary and are not cited; the repository page read gives the 2023 count.

## 11. Accidental

### 11.1 Africa: north, west and central

- I installed `pypdf` into the shared venv (`r1328/venv`) once by mistake and uninstalled it again; my own packages (pyshp, pypdf, openpyxl, shapely, pyproj, numpy, pyarrow, rasterio, pandas) are in a venv under my own scratch folder.
- Energydata downloads returned a Cloudflare challenge page (HTML saved as a .zip in my scratch; deleted).
- Otherwise none.

- My first overlap runs failed on shell word-splitting (zsh does not split unquoted variables) and on shapefiles with a single `Height` field; fixed and re-run. Nothing installed in any shared venv; my own venv is under my scratch folder. None otherwise.

### 11.2 Africa: east, southern and the islands

- A Cape Town query started in the background with the wrong box (an earlier edit via `sed -i` failed on macOS); it was stopped by its exact PID and rerun with a smaller box; the City server answered slowly (about 14 s a small query).
- `pip install pyshp pypdf` went into the shared venv given in the brief (not my own).
- Cloudflare challenge pages were saved as .zip files; nothing was done to get round them.
- Otherwise none.

### 11.3 Australia

none

### 11.4 New Zealand, the Pacific and Antarctica

A command with a TLS-check-off flag was refused by the permission classifier and not run; nothing was run with certificate verification off. A tool output echoed a very long SPREP page; nothing was lost. Otherwise none.

- One request to the Pacific Data Hub's alternate host was refused by the permission system and not run.

### 11.5 The PM's own run

- The sub-agents installed `pyshp`, `pypdf` and `openpyxl` into the shared virtual environment (afr-b by `pip install`, afr-a once by mistake, removed again); no repository file was touched.
- A background shell loop of mine (a Cape Town ArcGIS query at an offset past the end of the data, followed by a download) hung; it was stopped through the harness's task-stop on its own task, no process of another party was signalled. The download was repeated by hand and answered 200.
- Two tool calls were refused by the permission system (one sub-agent's attempt to use a curl flag that skips certificate checks, never run; one `sleep` I chained); no workaround was used.
- Cloudflare challenge pages were saved as .zip files by two sub-agents' downloads and deleted.
